Actor and creator coverage is complete for every candidate row, so both
columns always contain a number. Zero means no qualifying person was found
among the reviewed credits and bounded identity sources; it is not a negative
identity claim or proof that no other creator exists. Counts use person
evidence and never inherit identity from a role. Characters and Storylines
are intentionally omitted while actor and creator review is prioritized.

USA production uses explicit Wikipedia or Wikidata country-of-origin evidence.
USA citizens counts unique people among the row's qualifying Actors and
Creators who have a direct United States citizenship assertion; a person in
both categories counts once. Nationality and birthplace are not substitutes.

## 9. Review gate

Actor and creator counts are source-bounded and complete. Candidate membership
still requires review. Character and storyline review is intentionally deferred
and those columns are omitted from this focused report. Generation does not
itself approve candidate membership or publication.
